     129             : /**
     130             :  * READ
     131             :  * ====
     132             :  *
     133             :  * - Existing Library (#Main) push or free
     134             :  * - allocate new #Main
     135             :  * - load file
     136             :  * - read #SDNA
     137             :  * - for each LibBlock
     138             :  *   - read LibBlock
     139             :  *   - if a Library
     140             :  *     - make a new #Main
     141             :  *     - attach ID's to it
     142             :  *   - else
     143             :  *     - read associated 'direct data'
     144             :  *     - link direct data (internal and to LibBlock)
     145             :  * - read #FileGlobal
     146             :  * - read #USER data, only when indicated (file is `~/.config/blender/X.X/config/userpref.blend`)
     147             :  * - free file
     148             :  * - per Library (per #Main)
     149             :  *   - read file
     150             :  *   - read #SDNA
     151             :  *   - find LibBlocks and attach #ID's to #Main
     152             :  *     - if external LibBlock
     153             :  *       - search all #Main's
     154             :  *         - or it's already read,
     155             :  *         - or not read yet
     156             :  *         - or make new #Main
     157             :  *   - per LibBlock
     158             :  *     - read recursive
     159             :  *     - read associated direct data
     160             :  *     - link direct data (internal and to LibBlock)
     161             :  *   - free file
     162             :  * - per Library with unread LibBlocks
     163             :  *   - read file
     164             :  *   - read #SDNA
     165             :  *   - per LibBlock
     166             :  *     - read recursive
     167             :  *     - read associated direct data
     168             :  *     - link direct data (internal and to LibBlock)
     169             :  *   - free file
     170             :  * - join all #Main's
     171             :  * - link all LibBlocks and indirect pointers to libblocks
     172             :  * - initialize #FileGlobal and copy pointers to #Global
     173             :  *
     174             :  * \note Still a weak point is the new-address function, that doesn't solve reading from
     175             :  * multiple files at the same time.
     176             :  * (added remark: oh, i thought that was solved? will look at that... (ton)).
     177             :  */
